   92|       |    /// Appends an encoded event to the topic stream with Redis `XADD`.
   93|       |    ///
   94|       |    /// The returned acknowledgement means Redis accepted the command. It does
   95|       |    /// not guarantee that the record was fsynced or consumed. A connection loss
   96|       |    /// before the reply leaves the publish outcome unknown, so a retry may
   97|       |    /// append a duplicate.
   98|       |    ///

  111|       |    /// # Errors
  112|       |    ///
  113|       |    /// Returns an SPI error if the payload is not encoded, serialization fails,
  114|       |    /// admission is exhausted, a connection cannot be opened, or Redis rejects
  115|       |    /// `XADD`. A missing or malformed reply after send returns

  186|       |    /// Creates or reuses a consumer group and returns its async receiver.
  187|       |    ///
  188|       |    /// A group's cursor is created only when Redis has not seen the group
  189|       |    /// before. For an existing group, `New` or the explicit `resume` policy
  190|       |    /// retains its cursor; an explicit position otherwise returns an error.
  191|       |    /// Unsettled entries remain in the pending entries list when a read future

  339|       |    /// Completes shutdown without acknowledging any unsettled delivery.
  340|       |    ///
  341|       |    /// # Type Parameters
  342|       |    ///
  343|       |    /// - `'a`: Lifetime shared by the bus borrow and the returned future.

  354|       |    /// # Errors

   31|       |/// Factory registered for runtime-neutral asynchronous Redis Streams access.
   32|       |///
   33|       |/// The provider creates Redis clients and async SPI objects without choosing or
   34|       |/// starting an executor. The host application drives the returned futures.
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// # Examples
   37|       |///
   38|       |/// ```
   39|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::registry::EventBusConfig;
   40|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::AsyncEventBusSpi;
   41|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::r#async::AsyncRedisEventBusProvider;
   42|       |/// use qubit_spi::AsyncServiceProvider;

   44|       |/// # async fn example() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {

   52|       |/// # }
   53|       |/// # futures_lite::future::block_on(example()).expect("lazy provider creation succeeds");
   54|       |/// ```

  412|       |    /// Acknowledges terminal decisions and leaves retry decisions in PEL.
  413|       |    ///
  414|       |    /// Accept and Reject issue `XACK`; Retry only releases the receiver's local
  415|       |    /// in-flight slot, leaving the Redis entry available for redelivery.
  416|       |    /// Applying the same disposition again succeeds. Once XACK may have been
  417|       |    /// sent, cancellation or reply loss retains its intent and rejects
  418|       |    /// conflicts.
  419|       |    ///
  420|       |    /// # Parameters
  421|       |    ///
  422|       |    /// - `token`: Settlement token produced by this receiver.
  423|       |    /// - `disposition`: Terminal action or retry decision to apply.
  424|       |    ///
  425|       |    /// # Type Parameters
  426|       |    ///
  427|       |    /// - `'a`: Lifetime shared by the mutable receiver borrow and future.
  428|       |    ///
  429|       |    /// # Returns
  430|       |    ///
  431|       |    /// Success after local active-slot release and Applied bookkeeping.
  432|       |    /// Terminal decisions require a valid XACK reply (0 or 1); Retry commits
  433|       |    /// locally without acknowledging Redis. Repeating the applied intent is
  434|       |    /// idempotent, while unknown prior ACK intent remains fixed.
  435|       |    ///
  436|       |    /// # Errors

  439|       |    /// tokens, or an operation error if Redis settlement fails.

  781|       |                }
  782|       |            },

  784|      1|                *connection_reusable = false;
  785|      1|                None
  786|       |            }
  787|       |        }

                                            ^17
  792|     17|        message.with_provider_attempt(attempt)

 1122|       |    /// real PEL entry. The adapter must return its safe local commit error

 1124|       |    #[test]
 1125|      1|    fn test_settle_applied_xack_local_commit_failure_preserves_intent() -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>> {
 1126|      1|        block_on(async {

                                                           ^0
 1128|      1|            let proxy = ControlledRedis::start(server.url())?;

 1198|       |                    SettlementProgress::AckPending(DeliveryDisposition::Accept)

 1201|       |                // Internal fault injection targets local bookkeeping after real Redis ACK.
 1202|      1|                assert!(
 1203|      1|                    spawn(move || {
 1204|      1|                        let _guard = poisoned.lock().expect("recovery initially healthy");
 1205|      1|                        panic!("inject poisoned recovery between applied XACK and local commit");

 1224|      0|                panic!("local commit fault must map to a sanitized operation error");

 1227|      1|            assert_eq!(kind, "redis_error");
 1228|      1|            assert_eq!(retryable, None);

 1230|      1|                source.downcast_ref::<RedisProviderError>(),
 1231|      1|                Some(RedisProviderError::Operation("recovery lock"))

 1234|      1|                *progress.lock().expect("progress remains healthy"),

 1240|      1|                    .err()
 1241|      1|                    .expect("recovery remains poisoned")

 1244|       |                1
 1245|       |            );
 1246|      2|            for conflict in [DeliveryDisposition::Retry, DeliveryDisposition::Reject] {
                                          ^1
 1247|      2|                assert!(matches!(
                                      ^0
 1248|      2|                    subscription.settle(&token, conflict).await,
 1249|       |                    Err(SpiError::InvalidSettlementToken {
 1250|       |                        retryable: Some(false),
 1251|       |                        ..
 1252|       |                    })
 1253|       |                ));
 1254|       |            }
 1255|      1|            recovery.clear_poison();
 1256|       |            // The PEL was cleared above: this identical retry obtains XACK=0.
 1257|      1|            subscription.settle(&token, DeliveryDisposition::Accept).await?;

  633|       |    /// Local RESP endpoint recording setup and pipeline traffic; modes inject
  634|       |    /// non-PONG, ECHO disconnect, and PING disconnect without changing
  635|       |    /// production.
  636|       |    #[cfg(feature = "sync")]

  638|       |        url: String,

  758|       |    #[cfg(feature = "sync")]
  759|       |    impl Drop for LeaseEndpoint {
  760|       |        /// Stops acceptance and unblocks every socket before joining fixture
  761|       |        /// workers.
  762|       |        ///

    8|       |//! RAII ownership of one short application-operation admission slot.
    9|       |use std::sync::Arc;
   10|       |use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
   11|       |
   12|       |use super::resource_budget::ResourceBudget;
   13|       |/// Counter owned by a short-command permit.
   14|       |#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
   15|       |pub(super) enum CommandLane {
   16|       |    General,
   17|       |    ReservedSettlement,
   18|       |}
   19|       |/// Non-cloneable permit; cancellation releases admission, not Redis execution.
   20|       |#[must_use]
   21|       |pub(crate) struct CommandPermit {
   22|       |    /// Shared short-command counter decremented exactly once when this permit
   23|       |    /// is dropped.
   24|       |    pub(super) budget: Arc<ResourceBudget>,
   25|       |    /// Counter that was incremented when this permit was acquired.
   26|       |    pub(super) lane: CommandLane,
   27|       |}
   28|       |impl Drop for CommandPermit {
   29|       |    /// Releases exactly the slot acquired for this application operation.
   30|       |    #[inline]
   31|  3.87k|    fn drop(&mut self) {
   32|  3.87k|        match self.lane {
   33|  2.76k|            CommandLane::General => self.budget.commands.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel),
   34|  1.11k|            CommandLane::ReservedSettlement => self.budget.reserved_settlements.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel),
   35|       |        };
   36|  3.87k|    }
   37|       |}

   17|       |    /// No acknowledgement may have applied and no disposition is committed.

   19|       |    /// A terminal acknowledgement may have applied; only its intent may repeat.

    8|       |//! Shared Redis settlement metadata retained by synchronous and asynchronous
    9|       |//! tokens.
   10|       |
   11|       |use std::sync::Arc;
   12|       |use std::sync::Mutex;
   13|       |
   14|       |use qubit_event_bus::spi::DeliveryDisposition;
   15|       |

   18|       |
   19|       |/// Redis coordinates and shared settlement bookkeeping carried by one token.
   20|       |#[derive(Clone)]
   21|       |pub(crate) struct SettlementState {
   22|       |    /// Stream containing the pending event.
   23|       |    pub(crate) stream: String,
   24|       |    /// Consumer group that owns the pending entry.
   25|       |    pub(crate) group: String,

   31|       |    pub(crate) recovery: Arc<Mutex<RecoveryState>>,
   32|       |}
   33|       |
   34|       |impl SettlementState {
   35|       |    /// Commits `disposition` and releases its active slot without I/O.
   36|       |    ///
   37|       |    /// Locks progress before recovery, acquiring both before mutating either.
   38|       |    /// The caller must have validated this disposition and, for terminal
   39|       |    /// outcomes, received a valid XACK reply. Neither lock spans an await.
   40|       |    ///
   41|       |    /// # Parameters

   43|       |    /// - `disposition`: The permitted disposition to commit locally.
   44|       |    ///
   45|       |    /// # Returns
   46|       |    ///
   47|       |    /// Success once the active slot is released and progress becomes Applied.
   48|       |    ///
   49|       |    /// # Errors
   50|       |    ///
   51|       |    /// Returns a stable lock label when poisoning prevents the full commit.
   52|       |    /// Neither state changes; pending acknowledgement intent remains fixed
   53|       |    /// so only the identical terminal decision may retry.
   54|  1.09k|    pub(crate) fn commit(&self, disposition: DeliveryDisposition) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
   55|  1.09k|        let mut progress = self.progress.lock().map_err(|_| "settlement lock")?;
                          ^1.09k                                                            ^1
   56|  1.09k|        let mut recovery = self.recovery.lock().map_err(|_| "recovery lock")?;
                          ^1.08k                                                          ^3
   57|  1.08k|        if disposition == DeliveryDisposition::Retry {
   58|     15|            recovery.mark_retry(&self.message_id);
   59|  1.07k|        } else {
   60|  1.07k|            recovery.mark_terminal(&self.message_id);
   61|  1.07k|        }
   62|  1.08k|        *progress = SettlementProgress::Applied(disposition);
   63|  1.08k|        Ok(())
   64|  1.09k|    }

    8|       |//! Collision-safe Redis key construction.
    9|       |
   10|       |/// Builds a Redis stream key whose length-prefixed components cannot collide.
   11|       |///
   12|       |/// The namespace and topic are encoded as UTF-8 byte lengths followed by their
   13|       |/// original contents. This keeps component boundaries unambiguous even when a
   14|       |/// value contains `:`. The function does not validate either input; callers
   15|       |/// that accept user configuration should validate it before key construction.
   16|       |///
   17|       |/// # Parameters
   18|       |///
   19|       |/// - `namespace`: Application scope shared by related providers.
   20|       |/// - `topic`: Logical topic whose events are stored in the stream.
   21|       |///
   22|       |/// # Returns
   23|       |///
   24|       |/// A stable Redis key for this namespace and topic pair.
   25|       |///
   26|       |/// # Examples
   27|       |///
   28|       |/// ```
   29|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::naming::stream_key;
   30|       |///
   31|       |/// let key = stream_key("orders", "created");
   32|       |/// assert_eq!(key, "qubit:stream:6:orders:7:created");

   39|       |/// Builds a stable Redis consumer-group name from subscription identity.
   40|       |///
   41|       |/// When `group` is `None`, the subscriber ID becomes the group identity. An
   42|       |/// explicit group lets several subscribers share work while a different group
   43|       |/// receives its own copy of every stream entry. Component lengths count UTF-8
   44|       |/// bytes, so values containing `:` remain unambiguous.
   45|       |///
   46|       |/// # Parameters

   51|       |/// - `group`: Optional shared group identity.

   54|       |///

    8|       |//! Serialized quarantine transfer; Redis Lua does not roll back partial writes.
    9|       |
   10|       |#[cfg(feature = "sync")]
   11|       |use redis::ConnectionLike;
   12|       |use redis::ErrorKind;
   13|       |use redis::RedisError;
   14|       |use redis::ServerErrorKind;
   15|       |#[cfg(feature = "async")]
   16|       |use redis::aio::MultiplexedConnection;
   17|       |use redis::cmd;
   18|       |
   19|       |pub(crate) use crate::internal::PoisonOutcome;
   20|       |pub(crate) use crate::internal::PoisonReason;
   21|       |
   22|       |/// Lua preflights types and ownership, copies the last wire field, then ACKs.
   23|       |const QUARANTINE_SCRIPT: &str = r#"
   24|       |local source_type = redis.call('TYPE', KEYS[1]).ok
   25|       |if source_type == 'none' then return 0 end
   26|       |if source_type ~= 'stream' then return redis.error_reply('WRONGTYPE source must be a stream') end
   27|       |local quarantine_type = redis.call('TYPE', KEYS[2]).ok
   28|       |if quarantine_type ~= 'none' and quarantine_type ~= 'stream' then
   29|       |    return redis.error_reply('WRONGTYPE quarantine must be a stream')
   30|       |end
   31|       |local pending = redis.call('XPENDING', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[3], ARGV[3], 1)
   32|       |if #pending == 0 then return 0 end
   33|       |if pending[1][2] ~= ARGV[2] then return -1 end
   34|       |local rows = redis.call('XRANGE', KEYS[1], ARGV[3], ARGV[3])
   35|       |if #rows == 0 then
   36|       |    local acknowledged = redis.call('XACK', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[3])
   37|       |    if acknowledged == 1 then return 2 end
   38|       |    return 0
   39|       |end
   40|       |local fields = rows[1][2]
   41|       |local wire = ''
   42|       |local missing = '1'
   43|       |for index = 1, #fields, 2 do
   44|       |    if fields[index] == 'wire' then
   45|       |        wire = fields[index + 1]
   46|       |        missing = '0'
   47|       |    end
   48|       |end
   49|       |redis.call('XADD', KEYS[2], '*',
   50|       |    'source_stream', KEYS[1],
   51|       |    'source_id', ARGV[3],
   52|       |    'group', ARGV[1],
   53|       |    'reason', ARGV[4],
   54|       |    'wire', wire,
   55|       |    'wire_missing', missing)
   56|       |local acknowledged = redis.call('XACK', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[3])
   57|       |if acknowledged ~= 1 then return -2 end
   58|       |return 1
   59|       |"#;
   60|       |
   61|       |/// Transfers a malformed pending entry without interleaving other commands.
   62|       |///
   63|       |/// # Type Parameters
   64|       |///
   65|       |/// - `C`: Controlled blocking transport implementing Redis command execution.
   66|       |///
   67|       |/// # Parameters
   68|       |///
   69|       |/// - `connection`: Redis connection used for the single Lua command.
   70|       |/// - `source`: Stream key from which the malformed entry was read.
   71|       |/// - `quarantine`: Per-group quarantine stream key.
   72|       |/// - `group`: Consumer group whose pending entry is being settled.
   73|       |/// - `consumer`: Consumer that currently owns the pending entry.
   74|       |/// - `id`: Redis stream ID of the malformed entry.
   75|       |/// - `reason`: Stable decode failure category.
   76|       |///
   77|       |/// # Returns
   78|       |///
   79|       |/// A fixed transfer result; quarantined bytes are never returned to Rust.
   80|       |///
   81|       |/// # Errors
   82|       |///
   83|       |/// Returns a Redis error if the script cannot execute or returns an unknown
   84|       |/// status. Type and owner checks precede writes, but Redis Lua does not roll
   85|       |/// back an `XADD` when a subsequent `XACK` fails. A lost reply can hide a
   86|       |/// successful copy and acknowledgement; callers must preserve that uncertainty
   87|       |/// and inspect the source PEL before any later recovery attempt.
   88|       |#[cfg(feature = "sync")]

   90|     25|    connection: &mut C,

   94|     25|    consumer: &str,
   95|     25|    id: &str,
   96|     25|    reason: PoisonReason,

   98|     25|    let status: i64 = cmd("EVAL")
                      ^20     ^20
   99|     25|        .arg(QUARANTINE_SCRIPT)
  100|     25|        .arg(2)
  101|     25|        .arg(source)
  102|     25|        .arg(quarantine)
  103|     25|        .arg(group)

  105|     25|        .arg(id)
  106|     25|        .arg(reason.as_str())
  107|     25|        .query(connection)?;
                                        ^5
  108|     20|    match status {
  109|     18|        1 => Ok(PoisonOutcome::Quarantined),
  110|      1|        0 => Ok(PoisonOutcome::SourceGone),
  111|      1|        2 => Ok(PoisonOutcome::TombstoneCleared),
  112|      0|        -1 => Ok(PoisonOutcome::OwnershipChanged),
  113|      0|        _ => Err(RedisError::from((
  114|      0|            ErrorKind::Server(ServerErrorKind::ResponseError),
  115|      0|            "invalid quarantine result",
  116|      0|        ))),
  117|       |    }

  495|       |    /// # Parameters

  512|       |    /// Borrows the Redis URL used when creating a client.
  513|       |    ///
  514|       |    /// Provider-option parsing rejects a username or password embedded in the
  515|       |    /// URL; credentials are configured separately through environment
  516|       |    /// references.

  518|       |    /// # Returns
  519|       |    ///
  520|       |    /// The configured URL without allocating or exposing a mutable reference.
  521|       |    #[must_use]

  523|    399|    pub fn connection_url(&self) -> &str {

  525|    399|    }

   26|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::error::RedisProviderError;
   27|       |///
   28|       |/// let error = RedisProviderError::Configuration("invalid redis.url");
   29|       |/// assert_eq!(error.to_string(), "invalid Redis provider configuration: invalid redis.url");
   30|       |/// ```

   34|       |    /// A required option is missing or a supplied setting violates validation.
   35|       |    #[error("invalid Redis provider configuration: {0}")]
   36|       |    Configuration(
   37|       |        /// Static validation label that never contains endpoint or credential
   38|       |        /// values.
   39|       |        &'static str,
   40|       |    ),
   41|       |    /// A Redis operation failed and its raw client diagnostic was omitted.
   42|       |    #[error("Redis operation failed ({0})")]

   44|       |        /// Static operation label without raw Redis diagnostics or message
   45|       |        /// bytes.
   46|       |        &'static str,
   47|       |    ),
   48|       |    /// A Redis client operation failed with a stable, secret-safe category.

   50|       |    Transport {
   51|       |        /// Stable SPI operation name.
   52|       |        operation: &'static str,
   53|       |        /// Stable Redis error category.
   54|       |        kind: &'static str,
   55|       |        /// Retry policy when the Redis error kind is known.
   56|       |        retryable: Option<bool>,
   57|       |    },
   58|       |    /// A command may have executed but no valid reply confirmed its result.
   59|       |    #[error("Redis operation outcome unknown ({operation})")]
   60|       |    OutcomeUnknown {
   61|       |        /// Stable operation name, without connection or message data.
   62|       |        operation: &'static str,
   63|       |    },
   64|       |    /// A bounded resource admission limit was reached before sending a command.
   65|       |    #[error("Redis resource limit reached ({resource})")]
   66|       |    ResourceLimit {
   67|       |        /// Stable resource category, without user-supplied data.
   68|       |        resource: &'static str,
   69|       |    },

    8|       |//! Explicit durable subscription policy for Redis Streams consumers.
    9|       |
   10|       |use qubit_event_bus::model::ConsumerGroup;
   11|       |use qubit_event_bus::model::StartPosition;
   12|       |use qubit_event_bus::model::SubscribeOptions;
   13|       |use qubit_event_bus::model::SubscribeOptionsBuilder;
   14|       |use qubit_event_bus::model::SubscriptionDurability;
   15|       |
   16|       |/// Redis subscription settings shared across typed subscriber requests.
   17|       |///
   18|       |/// A start position is required for each profile. Redis subscriptions are
   19|       |/// durable; the returned builder can add ACK, retry, and middleware policies.
   20|       |/// Changing its durability to `Ephemeral` is rejected by the Redis provider's
   21|       |/// capability check when subscribing.
   22|       |///
   23|       |/// ```
   24|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::ConsumerGroup;
   25|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::StartPosition;
   26|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::SubscriptionDurability;
   27|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::RedisSubscriptionProfile;
   28|       |///
   29|       |/// let group = ConsumerGroup::new("billing")?;
   30|       |/// let options = RedisSubscriptionProfile::new(StartPosition::Earliest)
   31|       |///     .consumer_group(group.clone())
   32|       |///     .options::<String>()
   33|       |///     .build();
   34|       |/// assert_eq!(options.durability(), SubscriptionDurability::Durable);
   35|       |/// assert_eq!(options.start_position(), &StartPosition::Earliest);
   36|       |/// assert_eq!(options.consumer_group(), Some(&group));
   37|       |/// # Ok::<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>>(())
   38|       |/// ```
   39|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
   40|       |#[must_use]
   41|       |pub struct RedisSubscriptionProfile {
   42|       |    /// Required start position copied into each options builder.
   43|       |    start_position: StartPosition,
   44|       |    /// Optional consumer group applied to each options builder.
   45|       |    consumer_group: Option<ConsumerGroup>,
   46|       |}
   47|       |
   48|       |impl RedisSubscriptionProfile {
   49|       |    /// Creates a durable profile with the specified starting position and no
   50|       |    /// consumer group.
   51|       |    ///
   52|       |    /// `start_position` is passed unchanged to every options builder.
   53|       |    ///
   54|       |    /// # Parameters
   55|       |    ///
   56|       |    /// - `start_position`: Required position from which a new group should
   57|       |    ///   begin consuming, or the requested position when using an existing
   58|       |    ///   group.
   59|       |    ///
   60|       |    /// # Returns
   61|       |    ///
   62|       |    /// A durable profile that uses this start position and has no consumer
   63|       |    /// group configured.
   64|     10|    pub fn new(start_position: StartPosition) -> Self {
   65|     10|        Self {
   66|     10|            start_position,
   67|     10|            consumer_group: None,
   68|     10|        }
   69|     10|    }
   70|       |

   76|       |    /// - `group`: Consumer group to include in options built from this profile.
   77|       |    ///

   80|       |    /// The updated profile with `group` configured.
   81|       |    #[must_use = "Use the returned profile."]

   83|      8|        self.consumer_group = Some(group);
   84|      8|        self
   85|      8|    }
   86|       |
   87|       |    /// Builds durable options for payload type `T` with the profile's start
   88|       |    /// position and optional consumer group.
   89|       |    ///
   90|       |    /// Callers can append ACK, retry, and middleware settings before `build`.
   91|       |    /// A later `durability(Ephemeral)` call is rejected by the Redis provider
   92|       |    /// when the resulting request is subscribed.
   93|       |    ///
   94|       |    /// # Type Parameters

   96|       |    /// - `T`: Payload type delivered by the subscription.
   97|       |    ///
   98|       |    /// # Returns

  102|       |    /// before building the options.
  103|       |    #[must_use = "Build and apply these options to a subscription request."]
  104|     10|    pub fn options<T: 'static>(&self) -> SubscribeOptionsBuilder<T> {
  105|     10|        let options = SubscribeOptions::<T>::builder()
  106|     10|            .durability(SubscriptionDurability::Durable)
  107|     10|            .start_position(self.start_position.clone());
  108|     10|        match &self.consumer_group {

  110|      2|            None => options,
  111|       |        }

   28|       |/// Factory registered for synchronous Redis Streams access.
   29|       |///
   30|       |/// It validates provider options and creates the SPI implementation without
   31|       |/// opening a network connection. Short commands acquire admission-owning
   32|       |/// leases and reuse the bounded standalone idle pool; each receiver owns a
   33|       |/// dedicated connection until close or drop. Sentinel leases resolve the master
   34|       |/// afresh.
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// # Examples
   37|       |///
   38|       |/// ```
   39|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::registry::EventBusConfig;
   40|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::EventBusSpi;
   41|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::sync::RedisEventBusProvider;
   42|       |/// use qubit_spi::ServiceProvider;
   43|       |///
   44|       |/// let config = EventBusConfig::default();
   45|       |/// let bus = RedisEventBusProvider
   46|       |///     .create_configured(&config)
   47|       |///     .expect("default Redis settings are valid");
   48|       |/// let _capabilities = bus.capabilities();
   49|       |/// ```
   50|       |pub struct RedisEventBusProvider;
   51|       |

  135|       |    /// Recovers pending work without redelivering locally active entries.
  136|       |    ///
  137|       |    /// The first call and later calls after the recovery interval scan
  138|       |    /// reclaimable and own-pending entries before new group entries. Calls
  139|       |    /// before that deadline read new entries directly. Pending IDs already
  140|       |    /// held by local handlers are skipped. Malformed entries are copied to the
  141|       |    /// group quarantine stream and acknowledged by one serialized Lua
  142|       |    /// invocation. Lua does not roll back prior writes on failure, and a lost
  143|       |    /// reply leaves the transfer outcome unknown. Closing the receiver does
  144|       |    /// not acknowledge valid work.
  145|       |    ///
  146|       |    /// # Parameters

  423|       |    /// Acknowledges terminal dispositions and leaves retries in the PEL.
  424|       |    ///
  425|       |    /// Accept and Reject issue `XACK`; Retry only releases the receiver's local
  426|       |    /// in-flight slot and leaves the Redis entry pending. Repeating the same
  427|       |    /// disposition succeeds. Once XACK may have been sent, its intent remains
  428|       |    /// fixed across reply loss; conflicting dispositions are rejected.
  429|       |    ///
  430|       |    /// # Parameters
  431|       |    ///
  432|       |    /// - `token`: Settlement token produced by this receiver.
  433|       |    /// - `disposition`: Terminal action or retry decision to apply.
  434|       |    ///
  435|       |    /// # Returns
  436|       |    ///
  437|       |    /// Success after local active-slot release and Applied bookkeeping.
  438|       |    /// Terminal decisions require a valid XACK reply (0 or 1); Retry commits
  439|       |    /// locally without acknowledging Redis. Repeating the applied intent is
  440|       |    /// idempotent, while unknown prior ACK intent remains fixed.
  441|       |    ///
  442|       |    /// # Errors

  445|       |    /// tokens, or an operation error if Redis settlement fails.

   20|       |/// Dedicated receiver commands are covered by the receiver permit.
   21|       |pub(super) trait ReceiveCommand {
   22|       |    /// Sends a receiver command on a dedicated socket with bounded socket
   23|       |    /// waits.
   24|       |    ///
   25|       |    /// # Type Parameters
   26|       |    ///
   27|       |    /// - `T`: Owned reply type converted only after classifying a top-level
   28|       |    ///   rejection.
   29|       |    ///
   30|       |    /// # Parameters
   31|       |    ///
   32|       |    /// - `connection`: Dedicated receiver socket, discarded after uncertain
   33|       |    ///   failures.
   34|       |    ///
   35|       |    /// # Returns
   36|       |    ///
   37|       |    /// A typed owned response after bounded blocking I/O.
   38|       |    ///
   39|       |    /// # Errors
   40|       |    ///
   41|       |    /// Returns sanitized setup/top-level rejection or an unknown receive
   42|       |    /// outcome for I/O or malformed conversion replies.
   43|       |    fn query_receive<T: FromRedisValue>(&self, connection: &mut PooledConnection) -> Result<T, RedisProviderError>;
   44|       |}
   45|       |impl ReceiveCommand for Cmd {
   46|       |    /// Applies the receiver-command contract to Redis command bytes.
   47|       |    ///
   48|       |    /// # Type Parameters
   49|       |    ///
   50|       |    /// - `T`: Owned RESP conversion target.
   51|       |    ///
   52|       |    /// # Parameters
   53|       |    ///
   54|       |    /// - `connection`: Dedicated socket with finite waits.
   55|       |    ///
   56|       |    /// # Returns
   57|       |    ///
   58|       |    /// The converted owned reply.
   59|       |    ///
   60|       |    /// # Errors
   61|       |    ///
   62|       |    /// Returns setup errors, classified top-level rejections, or
   63|       |    /// outcome-unknown after uncertain I/O or nested/protocol failures.
   64|  1.80k|    fn query_receive<T: FromRedisValue>(&self, connection: &mut PooledConnection) -> Result<T, RedisProviderError> {
   65|  1.80k|        let raw = connection.req_command(self).map_err(|error| {
                          ^1.79k                                             ^7
   66|      7|            if error.is_response_too_large() {
   67|      4|                RedisProviderError::ResponseTooLarge
   68|       |            } else {
   69|      3|                RedisProviderError::OutcomeUnknown { operation: "receive" }
   70|       |            }
   71|      7|        })?;
   72|  1.79k|        if let Value::ServerError(error) = raw {
                                                ^8

   31|       |/// Clears deleted pending records while preserving the scan cursor and budget.
   32|       |///
   33|       |/// The claim reply's deleted-ID count is included in the result. Additional
   34|       |/// stale IDs are probed through bounded `XPENDING` and `XRANGE` reads, then
   35|       |/// cleared through the existing atomic quarantine script. A partial scan
   36|       |/// retains its cursor so a later receive can resume it.
   37|       |///
   38|       |/// # Parameters
   39|       |///
   40|       |/// - `subscription`: Receiver whose Redis keys, cursor state, and idle policy
   41|       |///   govern the scan.

   43|       |/// - `driver`: Per-receive command and maintenance budget.

   45|       |/// - `deleted_count`: Number of deleted IDs already reported by XAUTOCLAIM.
   46|       |///

   48|       |///
   49|       |/// The combined number of deleted pending records found in this scan.
   50|       |///
   51|       |/// # Errors

   55|       |
   56|       |/// Versioned event fields stored in one Redis Stream record.
   57|       |///
   58|       |/// The payload remains a byte vector, while headers are represented as JSON so
   59|       |/// the record can be round-tripped independently of a Rust process. Version 1
   60|       |/// carries encoded payloads and treats an unknown version as a protocol error.
   61|       |///
   62|       |/// # Examples
   63|       |///
   64|       |/// ```
   65|       |/// use std::sync::Arc;
   66|       |/// use std::time::SystemTime;
   67|       |///
   68|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::ContentType;
   69|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::EventId;
   70|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::model::Headers;
   71|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::EncodedPayload;
   72|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::OutboundMessage;
   73|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::TopicAddress;
   74|       |/// use qubit_event_bus::spi::TransportPayload;
   75|       |/// use qubit_event_bus_redis::wire::WireFields;
   76|       |///
   77|       |/// # fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   78|       |/// let message = OutboundMessage::new(
   79|       |///     TopicAddress::new("orders.created")?,
   80|       |///     EventId::new("evt-42")?,
   81|       |///     SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH,
   82|       |///     Headers::new(),
   83|       |///     None,
   84|       |///     None,
   85|       |///     TransportPayload::Encoded(EncodedPayload::new(
   86|       |///         Arc::from(&b"order-42"[..]),
   87|       |///         ContentType::new("text/plain")?,
   88|       |///         None,
   89|       |///     )),
   90|       |/// );
   91|       |/// let fields = WireFields::from_outbound(&message)?;
   92|       |/// assert_eq!(fields.version, 1);
   93|       |/// assert_eq!(fields.payload, b"order-42");
   94|       |/// # Ok(())
   95|       |/// # }
   96|       |/// ```
   97|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug, Deserialize, Serialize)]
   98|       |pub struct WireFields {
   99|       |    /// Protocol version used to select the field decoder.
  100|       |    pub version: u32,
  101|       |    /// Stable event identifier preserved across transport boundaries.
  102|       |    pub event_id: String,
  103|       |    /// Event timestamp as whole milliseconds since the Unix epoch.
  104|       |    pub timestamp_ms: u128,
  105|       |    /// Serialized string-valued event headers.
  106|       |    pub headers_json: String,
  107|       |    /// Optional ordering metadata; Redis does not enforce ordering by this key.
  108|       |    pub ordering_key: Option<String>,
  109|       |    /// MIME-like content type used by the facade codec registry.
  110|       |    pub content_type: String,
  111|       |    /// Optional schema identifier supplied by the encoder.
  112|       |    pub schema_id: Option<String>,
  113|       |    /// Encoded payload bytes, serialized as JSON integer values.
  114|       |    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
  115|       |}
  116|       |

  119|       |    ///
  120|       |    /// Native in-process payloads cannot be persisted and are rejected. The
  121|       |    /// timestamp must be at or after the Unix epoch.
  122|       |    ///
  123|       |    /// # Parameters

  125|       |    /// - `message`: Event whose encoded payload and metadata should be stored.

  131|       |    /// # Errors

  158|       |    /// Decodes version 1 fields into the typed components used by a stream
  159|       |    /// receiver.
  160|       |    ///
  161|       |    /// The topic is supplied by the consumer group because it is not duplicated
  162|       |    /// in each record. Invalid identifiers, headers, content types, and schema
  163|       |    /// identifiers fail decoding; timestamps are interpreted as milliseconds
  164|       |    /// after the Unix epoch and must fit the platform's `SystemTime` range.
  165|       |    /// Invalid optional ordering keys fail decoding instead of being discarded.
  166|       |    ///
  167|       |    /// # Parameters

  175|       |    /// supplied; `None` carries no ordering metadata. Missing schema
  176|       |    /// metadata stays absent.
  177|       |    ///
  178|       |    /// # Errors
  179|       |    ///
  180|       |    /// Returns `UnsupportedWireVersion` for unknown versions, a configuration
  181|       |    /// error for timestamps outside u64/platform range or an invalid ordering
  182|       |    /// key, and an operation error for malformed event ID, headers, content
  183|       |    /// type, or schema ID.

    8|       |//! Serialization sink that rejects writes before extending beyond its budget.
    9|       |
   10|       |use std::io::Error as IoError;

   18|       |
   19|       |/// Accumulates at most `limit` JSON bytes without performing external I/O.
   20|       |pub(super) struct BoundedWriter {
   21|       |    /// Serialized bytes accepted so far.
   22|       |    bytes: Vec<u8>,
   23|       |    /// Inclusive serialized byte budget.

   27|       |}

   35|       |    ///
